- **lint-zorch-srcs**: zorch-specific source-tree lint enforcing the two non-negotiables. Companion to /lint-zorch-docs — the same agnostic + fusion philosophy applied to code. Auto-detects subsystems from zorch/, so a new block inherits every check the moment its directory lands. - **lintcn**: Type-aware TypeScript lint rules in .lintcn/ Go files. Only load this skill when creating, editing, or debugging rule files. To just run the linter: `npx lintcn lint` (or `--fix`, `--tsconfig &lt;path&gt;`). Finds .lintcn/ by walking up from cwd. First build ~30s, cached ~1s. In monorepos, run from each package folder, not the root. Warnings don't fail CI and only show for git-changed files by default. Use `--all-warnings` to see them across the entire codebase.
